**[00:14:25.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000034)  
Good afternoon, distinguished colleagues, to the Secretary of the Department of Information

**[00:14:29.740] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000035)  
and Communications Technology, the heads and officials of the National Telecommunications

**[00:14:35.640] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000036)  
Commission, the Cybercrime Investigation and Coordinating Center, the National Privacy

**[00:14:41.640] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000037)  
Commission, as well as other officials and representatives joining us here today.

**[00:14:46.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000038)  
Good afternoon and welcome.

**[00:14:48.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000039)  
Today, we take up the fiscal year 2027 National Expenditures Program of the Department of

**[00:14:54.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000040)  
Information and Communications.

**[00:14:55.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000041)  
Technology and its attached agencies.

**[00:14:59.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000047)  
We assess this budget at an important juncture for the country. In just a few years, the government has built

**[00:15:05.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000048)  
a stronger legal foundation for a connected Philippines. The E-Governance Act advances

**[00:15:12.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000049)  
digital government and integrated public services. The Conectadong Pinoy Act seeks to expand access

**[00:15:19.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000050)  
and competition in data transmission and connectivity. But passing laws is only the

**[00:15:26.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000051)  
beginning. This budget must turn those laws into actual service. For me, the first test remains

**[00:15:34.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000052)  
connectivity. We will therefore be looking closely at the connectivity targets presented to Congress.

**[00:15:41.820] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000053)  
We want to know not only how many sites, access points, kilometers of fiber, or institutions are

**[00:15:48.740] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000054)  
reported as connected, but whether or not people on the ground are actually receiving reliable,

**[00:15:55.340] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000055)  
usable, and affordable services.

**[00:15:56.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000056)  
This is particularly important for our public schools. Connectivity in schools should mean more

**[00:16:05.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000057)  
than being able to just say that the school has been connected. We have to ask, is the connection

**[00:16:11.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000058)  
actually working? Is the bandwidth sufficient for teachers and students? Is it reliable throughout

**[00:16:19.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000059)  
the school day and cannot support digital learning, online government systems, and increasing

**[00:16:25.680] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000060)  
technological resources?

**[00:16:26.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000061)  
In short, we have to validate whether our target translates into real connectivity.

**[00:16:37.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000062)  
Our challenge is not just how many sites are connected to the report. The question is whether

**[00:16:45.220] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000063)  
teachers and students use it every day. And as we connect more Filipinos, we must also confront a

**[00:16:55.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000064)  
second question.

**[00:16:56.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000065)  
Where does our data go? Who controls the infrastructure carrying it? And how well are we

**[00:17:03.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000066)  
protecting it? Data sovereignty is no longer an abstract technological issue. Government is

**[00:17:09.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000067)  
digitizing records, transactions, identities, payments, and public services. We're generating

**[00:17:16.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000068)  
and processing enormous amounts of sensitive government data, citizen data. And so we need

**[00:17:23.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000069)  
to understand where critical government data is stored.

**[00:17:26.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000070)  
We need to understand where infrastructure is, what infrastructure carries it, and what safeguards apply when cloud and data services are provided by foreign entities and whether government has adequate control over information that is critical to national security and public administration.

**[00:17:42.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000071)  
This brings us to the expanding role of the DICT. The Department can no longer be viewed as a simple government agency responsible for internet connectivity and information technology projects.

**[00:17:56.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000073)  
In this new age, DICT sits at the intersection of connectivity, cybersecurity, artificial

**[00:18:04.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000074)  
intelligence, digital government, data governance, transparency, and economic development.

**[00:18:11.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000075)  
Cybersecurity is now national security.

**[00:18:14.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000076)  
Every government system we put online creates both an opportunity for better public service

**[00:18:19.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000077)  
and a potential for attack.

**[00:18:21.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000078)  
The question for this committee is whether we are investing enough, not only in systems

**[00:18:27.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000079)  
but in people, defenses, standards, and institutions to protect them.

**[00:18:33.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000080)  
Artificial intelligence presents another challenge.

**[00:18:35.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000081)  
AI can make government faster and more efficient.

**[00:18:39.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000082)  
It can support education, health, disaster response, agriculture, business, and many

**[00:18:45.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000083)  
other sectors.

**[00:18:46.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000084)  
But government must also be prepared for its risks.

**[00:18:50.940] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000085)  
From deepfakes to automated fraud,

**[00:18:51.940] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000086)  
to privacy issues, cybersecurity, accountability, and the possible disruption of Filipino jobs.

**[00:19:00.120] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000087)  
Our approach cannot simply be to fear AI or to regulate it without understanding.

**[00:19:06.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000088)  
The Philippines should be positioning itself to use AI, govern AI responsibly, and create

**[00:19:11.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000089)  
opportunities for Filipinos in AI-driven economies.

**[00:19:16.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000090)  
The same digital transformation should strengthen transparency.

**[00:19:20.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000091)  
Technology should make government easier to scrutinize, not harder.

**[00:19:25.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000092)  
Digital systems should allow citizens to know how public money is spent, how government

**[00:19:31.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000093)  
decisions are made, and whether agencies are delivering what they promised.

**[00:19:37.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000094)  
If we are spending billions of pesos to digitize the government, the greater accountability

**[00:19:43.340] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000095)  
and transparency should be among the returns of that investment.

**[00:19:49.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000096)  
Finally.

**[00:19:50.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000097)  
Digital policy is jobs policy.

**[00:19:53.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000098)  
Reliable broadband, competitive digital infrastructure, cloud services, cybersecurity, data centers,

**[00:20:00.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000099)  
digital platforms, and emerging technologies can attract investment and create higher-value

**[00:20:06.740] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000100)  
employment.

**[00:20:07.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000101)  
Our objective should not be to just make Filipinos consumers of technology.

**[00:20:13.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000102)  
We should be building conditions for Filipinos to become developers, cybersecurity experts,

**[00:20:19.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000103)  
engineers, entrepreneurs, innovators, and creators of technology.

**[00:20:24.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000104)  
Ang digital transformation ay hindi lang tungkol sa mas mabilis na internet.

**[00:20:29.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000105)  
Dapat may trabaho, negosyo, oportunidad at mas mahusay na serbisyo na kasunod nito.

**[00:20:35.500] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000106)  
But opportunity must come with trust.

**[00:20:41.640] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000107)  
As more Filipinos transact, communicate, work, study, and receive government services online,

**[00:20:47.400] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000108)  
we also have to make

**[00:20:49.400] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000110)  
digital space safer and more trustworthy. I want to raise one issue that goes directly to

**[00:20:56.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000111)  
that responsibility. Over the past year, several members of the House have sought

**[00:21:01.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000112)  
government assistance concerning social media content they reported as fake,

**[00:21:07.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000113)  
false, manipulated, defamatory, or otherwise harmful. These include requests

**[00:21:14.120] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000114)  
involving fabricated stories, altered images, AI-generated content, allegations

**[00:21:21.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000115)  
of corruption, personal attacks, and potentially threatening statements. Some

**[00:21:27.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000116)  
content was removed, but in a number of cases, the reported material remains

**[00:21:32.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000117)  
online even after referral, preservation, or review. One of the cases involves our

**[00:21:38.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000118)  
colleague, Congressman Miro Quimbo. His office alone referred 197 Facebook

**[00:21:44.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000119)  
links involving content directed against former Representative Stella Quimbo

**[00:21:49.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000120)  
and her family. According to the report furnished to us, these links were

**[00:21:52.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000121)  
monitored and validated, and the relevant digital material was preserved, but

**[00:21:58.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000122)  
reported content remained online after Meta found no violation of its

**[00:22:04.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000123)  
community standards. This raises a larger question which goes beyond the members

**[00:22:09.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000124)  
of Congress.

**[00:22:14.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000125)  
What happens? How does the Agency help? Do we have a solution? Sino mananagot sa proseso?

**[00:22:21.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000126)  
At paano natin sisiguraduhin na may malinaw, mabilis, at transparent na tugon dito?

**[00:22:33.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000127)  
Because if this happens to members of Congress, what more the ordinary Filipino?

**[00:22:38.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000128)  
We must make sure, and be equally clear, that government should not become an arbiter,

**[00:22:44.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000129)  
of what opinions people may express online. Criticism of public officials is part of democratic

**[00:22:50.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000130)  
life, but manipulated media, impersonation, fraud, threats, coordinated deceptive activity,

**[00:22:59.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000131)  
and other unlawful online conduct present a different problem, and require effective

**[00:23:05.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000132)  
institutional response, consistent with freedom of expression and due process. And for that

**[00:23:12.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000133)  
reason, I am formally

**[00:23:13.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000134)  
turning over

**[00:23:14.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000135)  
to the DICT

**[00:23:15.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000136)  
and the Cybercrime

**[00:23:16.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000137)  
Investigation

**[00:23:17.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000138)  
Coordinating

**[00:23:18.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000139)  
Center, the consolidated complaints supporting materials received from members

**[00:23:23.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000140)  
from the House. And I am likewise furnishing META a copy for its appropriate review.

**[00:23:29.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000141)  
I ask the DICT and CIC to review these cases individually, and to report to this committee

**[00:23:38.400] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000142)  
on what action has been taken and what remains pending, what further action may be taken,

**[00:23:44.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000143)  
within their respective mandates and what legal technical institutional or

**[00:23:49.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000144)  
policy gaps may be preventing an effective response we also request meta

**[00:23:56.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000145)  
or we are requesting method to review the reported content under its

**[00:24:01.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000146)  
applicable policies and explain the disposition of referrals where

**[00:24:06.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000147)  
appropriate and clarify for review or escalation mechanisms that are available

**[00:24:12.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000148)  
when reported content is found not to violate its community standards and

**[00:24:18.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000149)  
since representatives of meta have been requested also to attend this hearing I

**[00:24:22.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000150)  
hope we can hear from them directly as I cannot place the blame solely on CICC

**[00:24:27.940] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000151)  
and DICT for not being able to respond to many of the complaint complaints right

**[00:24:33.780] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000152)  
away not just from members of Congress but from members all over government as

**[00:24:38.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000153)  
well as from private sector in civil society

**[00:24:42.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000154)  
so

**[00:24:42.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000155)  
we examine the fiscal year twenty twenty seven budget and I would like to and I

**[00:24:49.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000156)  
would like the department to satisfy this committee on several fundamental

**[00:24:52.780] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000157)  
questions are our connectivity targets real measurable and felt on the ground

**[00:24:58.340] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000158)  
particularly in our schools in underserved communities are we

**[00:25:02.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000159)  
adequately protecting government systems critical infrastructure and Filipino

**[00:25:07.780] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000160)  
data do we have clear strategy for cybersecurity and artificial

**[00:25:12.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000161)  
intelligence, including the opportunities for Filipino workers and businesses?

**[00:25:17.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000162)  
Are our investments in digital government producing greater transparency,

**[00:25:24.120] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000163)  
accountability, and better public service?

**[00:25:27.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000164)  
And last, do our institutions have the capability, legal tools,

**[00:25:33.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000165)  
to respond effectively to new risks that come with an increasingly digital society,

**[00:25:40.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000166)  
especially for young people?

**[00:25:43.400] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000167)  
These are not separate issues.

**[00:25:45.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000168)  
These are all part of the same national project.

**[00:25:49.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000169)  
Connect the country, protect its data, secure its systems,

**[00:25:53.500] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000170)  
use technology to make government more transparent,

**[00:25:56.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000171)  
and make sure that the digital economy creates opportunities for Filipinos.

**[00:26:02.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000172)  
The trust of the people should be equal to the service.

**[00:26:08.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000173)  
And the connection should not just be

**[00:26:10.500] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000174)  
fast, but safe.

**[00:26:14.120] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000175)  
With that, I now invite the Department to present its Fiscal Year 2027 proposal in detail.

**[00:26:22.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000176)  
After the presentation, we will hear questions, concerns, and views of our colleagues.

**[00:26:27.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000177)  
Thank you very much.

**[00:27:42.910] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000194)  
Our direction for Fiscal Year 2027 is

**[00:27:47.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000198)  
anchored on the DICT strategic framework.

**[00:27:50.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000199)  
Para mas madali pong maalala.

**[00:27:53.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000200)  
D is for Digital First Economy.

**[00:27:56.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000201)  
We want to equip Filipinos with the skills, opportunities, and enabling environment they need to participate and succeed in an increasingly digital economy.

**[00:28:06.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000202)  
I is for Inclusive Tech for All.

**[00:28:09.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000203)  
Digital transformation must reach everyone.

**[00:28:12.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000204)  
This means expanding reliable and affordable connectivity

**[00:28:18.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000207)  
and bringing digital infrastructure

**[00:28:19.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000208)  
and services closer to

our communities,

**[00:28:21.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000210)  
lalong-lalo na po

sa mga kababayan

**[00:28:23.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000212)  
natin

**[00:28:24.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000213)  
sa GIDA areas. C is for championing cybersecurity and data privacy. As more Filipinos

**[00:28:33.530] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000214)  
businesses and government services move online, we must make our digital environment safer,

**[00:28:40.290] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000215)  
more resilient, and more trusted. Connectivity and digitalization must always go hand in hand

**[00:28:47.070] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000216)  
with cyber security and protection of our people's data. At kamakailan po, mainit-init

**[00:28:52.650] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000217)  
ang usipin natin sa social media at sa mga NVE situation po. Kabilin-bilinan ng ating

**[00:28:59.590] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000218)  
Pangulo, walang kabataan na dapat maiwan at walang kabataan na dapat mapahamak.

**[00:29:06.570] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000219)  
And T is for transparency and governance. We want technology to make government simpler,

**[00:29:12.130] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000220)  
faster, and more transparent, and more responsive to our citizens so that our citizens spend

**[00:29:20.130] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000221)  
less time navigating government processes.

**[00:29:22.650] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000222)  
And more time benefiting from the services of what government provides. Kaya po nandyan

**[00:29:28.910] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000223)  
po ang ating E-Gov app, nandyan po ang E-LGU, at marami pang mga sistema para hindi na

**[00:29:35.950] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000224)  
pumipila ang ating mga kababayan, kundi nasiservisuhan na mas mabilis. Because ultimately,

**[00:29:41.810] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000225)  
digital transformation is not simply about technology, it is about people. Ang tunay

**[00:29:46.910] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000226)  
na sukatan nito ay kung maaabot at nararamdaman ba ng ating mga kababayan ang benepisyon ng

**[00:29:52.650] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000227)  
teknolohiya. And before we proceed to the numbers and programs, we would first like

**[00:29:57.850] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000228)  
to hear from some of the Philippine

**[00:30:00.000] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000229)  
At the heart of our work, may maiksi lang pong AVP na papakita. Maiksi lang naman po yan, Mr. Chair.

**[00:30:27.430] Presentation video** (asr-000230)  
Ang present natin ngayon is halos lahat nangyayari nasa internet. Kapag wala kang internet, parang mapag-iiwanan ka.

**[00:30:35.410] Presentation video** (asr-000231)  
Ang pre-Wi-Fi po sa amin ay bihirang bihira po. Kami po ay kasalukuyang kumukonekta gamit po ng data sa aming mga telepono.

**[00:30:44.010] Presentation video** (asr-000232)  
Actually, before po, nung wala pa pong DICT po, yung ginagawa po ng mga tao po dito, pumupunta pa po sila sa taas ng hundok po.

**[00:30:52.530] Presentation video** (asr-000233)  
Without centralized services, agencies would need to make independent investments in hardware, which is quite expensive.

**[00:30:59.730] Presentation video** (asr-000234)  
Sa loob ng ilang linggo, paggamit namin ng DICT by ANC, nagdala ito ng pagbabago.

**[00:31:06.190] Presentation video** (asr-000235)  
Lalo lalo na sa aming pagtuturo, mas naging engaging ang aming talakayan.

**[00:31:12.370] Presentation video** (asr-000236)  
Mas aktibo na nakikilabok ang amin.

**[00:31:14.010] Presentation video** (asr-000237)  
Ang aming mga mag-aaral.

**[00:31:15.290] Presentation video** (asr-000238)  
These services deliver value far beyond subscription-based infrastructure.

**[00:31:20.390] Presentation video** (asr-000239)  
They enable government agencies like ours, particularly those with limited resources and manpower, to actively participate in digital transformation.

**[00:31:29.090] Presentation video** (asr-000240)  
This kind of communication equipment is going to help people communicate in a time of crisis.

**[00:31:35.270] Presentation video** (asr-000241)  
One of the biggest challenges is to know where to send help, who needs the help, and how to get it to people.

**[00:31:42.270] Presentation video** (asr-000242)  
Malaking tulong talaga siya sa amin, and I also hope that this project can be expanded farther, and that internet connectivity will continue to improve, especially in farther barangays where the signal is limited.

**[00:31:56.910] Presentation video** (asr-000243)  
This is going to be the success factor that is going to save lives and change lives.

**[00:32:02.110] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000244)  
Yan po isang maiksing video lang para sa DICT.

**[00:32:18.470] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000245)  
Bago po namin puntahan ng mga numero,

**[00:32:22.350] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000246)  
bigyan ko lang po siguro ako ng konting personal na mensahe po ng pasasalamat.

**[00:32:27.910] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000247)  
Pasasalamat po dahil pangalawang budget hearing ko na po ito.

**[00:32:31.450] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000248)  
Una po sa Pangulo, sa kanyang vision at sa kanyang suporta sa DICT,

**[00:32:37.210] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000249)  
sa mga sumusuporta po namin sa buong kongreso po,

**[00:32:40.570] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000250)  
malaking bagay po ang mga batas na naipasan nyo.

**[00:32:43.530] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000251)  
Yung konektadong Pinoy, malaking nagawa po niya sa industriya ng telecommunications.

**[00:32:49.230] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000252)  
Hindi man siya nakasama sa budget namin.

**[00:32:52.350] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000253)  
Pero gusto ko pong ihayag sa committee na nasa 2.4 billion US dollars po

**[00:32:58.130] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000254)  
ang committed investment ng telecoms industry ngayong taon po.

**[00:33:02.790] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000255)  
Yan po ay nasa between the 2 to 3 billion na inaasahan natin na manggagaling sa kanila.

**[00:33:08.270] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000256)  
Ang pagpapasa nyo po ng e-governance act na ngayon ay magiging full speed ahead na po.

**[00:33:14.590] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000257)  
Yan po ang magpapadali at magiging efficient po ang servisyo ng ating pamahalaan.

**[00:33:22.350] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000258)  
At sa mga oversight committees na lahukan po namin,

**[00:33:27.710] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000259)  
umaasa po kami yung mga susunod na batas,

**[00:33:29.810] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000260)  
aayos po yung mga iba pa nating mga mas matitinding haharapin sa cyber security, social media, etc.

**[00:33:37.730] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000261)  
Sa mga kasama ko po sa DICT at ang mga attached agency, CICC, NPC, NTC,

**[00:33:45.510] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000262)  
tumataas po ang kumpiyansa ko sa servisyo ng mga tao sa gobyerno.

**[00:33:52.350] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000263)  
Masisipag, magagalang, at professional po ang mga tao sa DICT.

**[00:33:57.330] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000264)  
Lahat po ay pwede nyo lapitan at lahat po ay hindi tatanggi sa pagsisilbi sa bayan.

**[00:34:05.550] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000265)  
Doon po sa ating mga kababayan, lalo na po yung mga matagal nang naghihintay ng connectivity.

**[00:34:13.090] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000266)  
Salamat po sa inyong pagtangkilik sa servisyo ng DICT.

**[00:34:17.030] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000267)  
Salamat po at alam namin naghintay kayo ng matagal.

**[00:34:20.470] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000268)  
At kayo po ang dahilan kung bakit ngayong budget season na ito,

**[00:34:25.690] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000269)  
bawat piso na hihingin namin sa Kongreso ay ilalaan namin sa pagsisilbisyo sa inyo.

**[00:34:32.570] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000270)  
Afterwards, with the permission of the Chair,

**[00:34:35.270] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000271)  
I will now turn over the detailed presentation to our Undersecretary for Support Services,

**[00:34:41.470] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000272)  
Undersecretary Heherson Asiddao,

**[00:34:44.030] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000273)  
who will present highlights of the Department's accomplishment in our proposed budget

**[00:34:48.650] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000274)  
for Fiscal Year 2027

**[00:34:50.470] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000275)  
that is embodied in our NEP.

**[00:34:53.790] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000276)  
Again, maraming maraming salamat po.

**[00:34:55.830] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000277)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[00:34:57.470] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000278)  
Usec. Asiddao, you're recognized.

**[00:35:00.750] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000279)  
Thank you very much, Sec. Henry.

**[00:35:02.830] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000280)  
With your permission, Mr. Chair and the honorable members of this committee,

**[00:35:06.650] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000281)  
a pleasant afternoon to everyone.

**[00:35:08.870] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000282)  
Allow us first to highlight some of the landmark accomplishments and reforms

**[00:35:12.850] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000283)  
that are already translating our investments in ICT into tangible benefits for the Filipino people.

**[00:35:20.470] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000285)  
Mr. Chair, a copy of our full-page presentation is provided to each member of this committee for your perusal.

**[00:35:28.270] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000286)  
I will go over the landmark accomplishments and at the same time the detailed NEP for 2027.

**[00:35:34.030] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000287)  
Mr. Chair, one of the most significant accomplishments is bringing government services

**[00:35:39.670] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000288)  
directly into the hands of millions of Filipinos through the eGov Super App.

**[00:35:44.030] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000289)  
Today, we are proud to say that we have 61 million plus downloads,

**[00:35:49.790] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000291)  
More than 56 million verified accounts and over 1.2 billion eGovDX transactions

**[00:35:54.470] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000292)  
with 1,332 government e-services already integrated.

**[00:35:59.430] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000293)  
These include everyday services such as PhilHealth, eTravel, eCov, eTrabajo, eLGU, and eReport,

**[00:36:07.970] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000294)  
alongside more than 92 million digital national IDs issued and over 300 million digital ID transactions.

**[00:36:14.610] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000295)  
More importantly, digital government is now institutionalized through the

**[00:36:19.790] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000296)  
e-Governance Act and its Implementing Rules and Regulations.

**[00:36:23.750] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000297)  
Our direction is clear.

**[00:36:25.430] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000298)  
Instead of citizens having to navigate government,

**[00:36:28.230] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000299)  
we are bringing government closer to the citizen through one increasingly integrated digital ecosystem.

**[00:36:36.890] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000300)  
Alongside expanding digital services, we have pursued reforms that make it faster and easier

**[00:36:42.890] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000301)  
to build telecommunications infrastructure, ranging from streamlined permitting

**[00:36:47.370] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000302)  
and longer regulatory validity to infrastructure sharing and open access.

**[00:36:51.970] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000303)  
These reforms help enable expansion of telecommunications towers from close to 18,000 in 2020 to about 35,000 in 2023,

**[00:37:03.610] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000304)  
while the number of connected Filipinos increased from 77 million in July 2022 to about 99 million by June of 2026.

**[00:37:13.610] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000305)  
With the help of the Konektadong Pinoy Act, we are building on these gains by promoting greater competition,

**[00:37:19.610] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000306)  
infrastructure sharing, and more efficient network deployment.

**[00:37:21.970] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000307)  
The objective is straightforward.

**[00:37:25.710] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000308)  
As government becomes more digital, connectivity must expand with it so more Filipinos can actually participate and benefit.

**[00:37:36.220] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000309)  
These infrastructure and policy reforms are translating into measurable improvements in the everyday internet experience of Filipinos.

**[00:37:43.940] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000310)  
Since 2022, mobile download speeds have more than doubled, while fixed broadband speeds have also improved.

**[00:37:50.800] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000311)  
At the same time, Filipinos are getting significantly

**[00:37:54.180] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000312)  
more data for every peso spent.

**[00:37:56.920] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000313)  
Most importantly, Mr. Chair, access is expanding.

**[00:37:59.920] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000314)  
The share of Filipinos using internet increased from 25.4% in 2019 to about 67% in 2024,

**[00:38:09.420] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000315)  
while households with internet access increased from 17.7% to about 48.8%.

**[00:38:15.580] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000316)  
So the story across these first three accomplishments is connected.

**[00:38:20.360] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000317)  
We are digitalizing government, removing barriers to connectivity,

**[00:38:24.180] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000318)  
and working to ensure that faster and more affordable internet allows more Filipinos to actually participate in our digital transformation.

**[00:38:33.180] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000319)  
And we are applying that same principle of digital transformation to improve specific government services.

**[00:38:40.180] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000320)  
When better connectivity is paired with digital government, we can transform even very specific services that directly affect people's livelihoods.

**[00:38:51.740] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000321)  
A good example is the PIMEDES portal,

**[00:38:53.740] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000322)  
Mr. Chair.

**[00:38:55.740] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000323)  
What used to take an average of three months through a manual process can now be completed digitally in about five days.

**[00:39:02.740] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000324)  
As of May 2026, Mr. Chair,

**[00:39:04.620] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000325)  
1,400 drivers have registered nationwide.

**[00:39:12.010] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000326)  
[unclear] open [unclear] manual [unclear].

**[00:39:19.450] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000327)  
[unclear] monthly [unclear] by [unclear]%.

**[00:39:26.860] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000328)  
[unclear] 1,000 [unclear] 167 [unclear] digital [unclear].

**[00:39:39.410] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000329)  
[unclear] spent on [unclear].

**[00:39:44.670] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000330)  
More [unclear] services [unclear].

**[00:39:47.470] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000331)  
[unclear] also ensure [unclear] environment [unclear].

**[00:39:50.870] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000332)  
[unclear]

**[00:39:54.140] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000333)  
As more services [unclear].

**[00:39:56.860] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000334)  
[unclear]

**[00:39:58.900] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000335)  
[unclear] even more [unclear].

**[00:40:01.260] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000336)  
[unclear] center.

**[00:40:06.720] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000338)  
We funded coverage from just 10 government agencies

**[00:40:11.020] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000339)  
in 2022

**[00:40:11.520] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000340)  
to 99

**[00:40:11.980] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000341)  
agencies by August 2026,

**[00:40:14.400] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000342)  
providing continuous

**[00:40:15.280] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000343)  
cyber monitoring

**[00:40:16.280] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000344)  
across government.

**[00:40:19.700] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000345)  
To date, Mr. Chair, the NSOC has monitored about 6.7 billion security alerts, demonstrating both the scale of threats we face and the importance of maintaining a strong national cyber defense capability.

**[00:40:32.700] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000346)  
Our investment in the next-generation hybrid NSOC will therefore sustain and strengthen 24-7 protection across disconnected agencies. Because our digital transformation can only succeed if Filipinos can also trust that the government systems and services they increasingly depend on are secure and resilient.

**[00:40:53.320] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000347)  
But a secure and connected digital Philippines is only meaningful if Filipinos also have the skills to participate in it.

**[00:41:03.190] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000348)  
From 2022 to 2026,

**[00:41:04.850] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000349)  
DICT has reached about 695,000 Filipinos through free and accessible ICT capacity development, providing free training in areas such as AI, emerging technologies, cybersecurity, digital work, entrepreneurship, and digital literacy.

**[00:41:25.190] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000350)  
Through our DTCs, or Digital Transformation Centers, and initiatives such as SPARC, we are also bringing these opportunities closer to communities.

**[00:41:34.850] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000351)  
Including support that has helped form 35 local freelancer organizations nationwide.

**[00:41:42.670] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000352)  
Our goal is to complete the chain, connect our people, protect them online, equip them with relevant digital skills, and ultimately open more opportunities for work, enterprise, and participation in digital economy.

**[00:41:56.570] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000353)  
As we expand these programs, we are also continuously improving how we deliver them.

**[00:42:05.840] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000354)  
A good example is our free public internet access program.

**[00:42:08.300] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000355)  
Through improved technology, contracting, and connectivity arrangement, we have achieved reported cost efficiencies of up to 70%, Mr. Chair, while continuing to expand access, particularly in geographically isolated and disadvantaged areas.

**[00:42:26.620] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000356)  
Today, free Wi-Fi has reached about 10.37 million unique beneficiaries with 127.49 million user sessions.

**[00:42:35.240] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000357)  
At an estimated cost of about 22.4 pesos per unique beneficiary, and that's per month.

**[00:42:43.120] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000358)  
This reflects how we want the ICT to work.

**[00:42:46.380] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000359)  
Expand what delivers, impact, measure performance, learn from implementation, and continuously find ways to deliver more value for every peso entrusted to us.

**[00:42:58.360] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000360)  
And that is the principle we will carry moving forward.

**[00:43:01.020] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000361)  
Not simply to do more, but to do better.

**[00:43:05.080] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000362)  
More efficiently, more effectively, and with greater success.

**[00:43:08.300] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000363)  
And with greater impact for the Filipino people.

**[00:43:12.590] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000364)  
Mr. Chair, I will now proceed to the Fiscal Year 2027 National Expenditure Program and present how the proposed budget will support the Department's priorities and commitments for the coming year.

**[00:43:26.170] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000365)  
For the Fiscal Year 2027, the proposed budget for the ICT and its attached agencies totals 19.491 billion.

**[00:43:34.070] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000366)  
Of this amount, 17.6 billion is for the DICT Office of the Secretary.

**[00:43:40.190] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000367)  
While 594.4 million is provided for CICC, 505.4 million for the National Privacy Commission, and 790 million for the National Telecommunications Commission.

**[00:43:56.170] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000368)  
Across the entire department, the budget consists of 1.692 billion for personal services, 13.9 billion for MOE, and 3.892 billion for capital outlay.

**[00:44:10.190] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000369)  
In closing, Mr. Chair, and the honorable members of this committee, what we have presented today is more than a list of programs, targets, and budget figures.

**[00:44:20.090] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000370)  
Ito po ang direksyon ng DICT, mula connectivity, digital government, cybersecurity, digital skills, and jobs, hanggang sa pagbuo ng isang mas modernong gobyerno na tunay na naramdaman ng ating mga kababayan.

**[00:44:34.530] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000371)  
With the support of the Congress for our Fiscal Year 2027 budget, we commit to translate every peso.

**[00:44:40.070] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000373)  
entrusted to DICT into better connectivity, better digital services, greater digital opportunities, and concrete benefits for every Filipino.

**[00:44:51.330] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-000374)  
Maraming salamat po, and magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat.

**[00:44:55.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000375)  
Alright, maraming salamat po.

**[00:45:04.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000376)  
Okay, with that, we will now go on to allowing our colleagues here to ask their questions of the department regarding the NEP for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:45:14.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000377)  
May I remind our distinguished colleagues that each member is allotted five minutes for questions or interpolation, excluding the response of the resource persons.

**[00:45:25.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000378)  
When the bell sounds, the member has 30 seconds to conclude.

**[00:45:29.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000379)  
Consistent with the long-standing practice of the House, the Chair shall recognize the minority, independent, and majority members in an alternating manner when practicable.

**[00:45:42.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000380)  
The Chair first recognizes the Honorable Renee Ko from the minority.

**[00:45:54.680] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000381)  
Ah, dapat, pero yung una nawala eh.

**[00:45:56.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000382)  
So, ladies first, as they say.

**[00:46:00.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000383)  
Mr. Chair, thank you very much.

**[00:46:02.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000384)  
And good afternoon.

**[00:46:02.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000385)  
Good afternoon to the Department of Information and Communication Technology.

**[00:46:07.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000386)  
My question would first be in relation to RA 10929, or the Free Public Internet Access Act.

**[00:46:18.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000387)  
Last year, we took note of your report, and we also find it in the handout that the department gave us.

**[00:46:26.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000388)  
That they targeted, or the department targeted, 10.2 million.

**[00:46:32.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000389)  
individual beneficiaries for 2026, and then supposedly 4.5 million users for 2027.

**[00:46:42.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000390)  
Can I check if that's correct?

**[00:46:47.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000391)  
From last year, you mentioned that the target is 15.8 million individual beneficiaries.

**[00:46:54.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000392)  
This was set in 2025.

**[00:46:56.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000393)  
May I ask kung ano na pong progress, and ano pong nilalaman ng, supposedly, pag-targeting?

**[00:47:02.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000394)  
ng beneficiaris ng RA 10929 in this manner?

**[00:47:09.740] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000395)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor, I would like to ask my colleague, Asek Chris, to respond with a detailed answer for your question, if it's okay, Mr. Chair.

**[00:47:26.120] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000396)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair.

**[00:47:28.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000397)  
Yes, you may be recognized.

**[00:47:29.820] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000398)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair.

**[00:47:32.860] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000399)  
Currently, Mr. Chair, we have about 10.37 million unique users for the August 31 data, and that encompasses 11,836 different locations, composed of schools, markets, tourist spots, public places, barangay halls, and other public plazas, with 29,091 operational access points.

**[00:47:59.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000400)  
Mr. Chair.

**[00:48:00.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000401)  
Correct me if I'm wrong na lang.

**[00:48:05.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000402)  
Tinak-count niyo po kung ilan po yung nakakakonect sa free public internet, and yun yung characterization natin ng beneficiaris.

**[00:48:14.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000403)  
Is this correct, Mr. Chair?

**[00:48:15.780] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000404)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[00:48:17.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000405)  
So, Mr. Chair, last year, you mentioned na may 15.8 million target beneficiaris kayo.

**[00:48:23.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000406)  
So, I assume because ang report niyo po ay 10.3 million this year, hindi po natin na-meet yung target na yun.

**[00:48:30.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000407)  
So, can I ask po ba, paano niyo po ba ino-operationalize?

**[00:49:00.600] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000408)  
Ito nga po, ma'am. Patray nga po itong wireless.

**[00:49:03.000] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000409)  
Sorry po.

**[00:49:05.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000410)  
Connectivity issue, right now.

**[00:49:08.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000411)  
Ayan, sige.

**[00:49:11.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000412)  
Ayan, so paano niyo po na may measure yung gusto nating 15 million na target beneficiaris?

**[00:49:18.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000413)  
Will you just add, essentially, access points?

**[00:49:21.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000414)  
And paano may ensure na yung additional 5 million ay maka-access to these access points?

**[00:49:30.650] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000415)  
Mr. Chair.

**[00:49:31.710] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000416)  
For now po, we aim to achieve the 15 million unique users by the end of this year.

**[00:49:37.650] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000417)  
So, we have a lot of projects right now that are in the pipeline and are nearing the post-qual evaluation and subsequent award.

**[00:49:48.090] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000418)  
With this po, pag nag-start na po mag-deployment, we will be deploying additional access points in different locations

**[00:49:54.710] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000419)  
that covers po yung mga public markets, tourist spots, and additional schools po.

**[00:50:01.170] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000420)  
Ati po mga rural health units po natin.

**[00:50:04.630] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000421)  
So, that's how we aim to achieve yung 15 million target po natin.

**[00:50:09.950] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000422)  
So, maintain po yung 15.8 million target beneficiaris for this year also?

**[00:50:15.650] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000423)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[00:50:16.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000424)  
Okay, and by adding access points.

**[00:50:19.330] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000425)  
Can I ask po, because under the law, may minimum na sineset na sana po yung public basic education institutions and state universities and colleges ay makabitan.

**[00:50:29.910] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000426)  
Can I ask for...

**[00:50:31.010] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000427)  
Progress update po dito.

**[00:50:33.150] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000428)  
Ilan po bang...

**[00:50:34.250] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000429)  
How many percentage of public educational institutions and SUCs ang nakabitan na po natin?

**[00:50:41.740] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000430)  
Yung sa...

**[00:50:42.800] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000431)  
For the...

**[00:50:43.480] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000432)  
Mr. Chair, yung for sa DepEd schools po muna unahin ko.

**[00:50:47.280] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000433)  
Yung sa schools connected po natin, based on our latest data,

**[00:50:50.560] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000434)  
last August 31, 99.39% na po ang schools out,

**[00:50:56.280] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000435)  
considering po yung mga DepEd schools na 45,328.

**[00:50:59.540] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000436)  
So, 99.39% na po yung connected.

**[00:51:02.420] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000437)  
And we also have yung...

**[00:51:03.740] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000438)  
Sa SUCs naman po, meron po tayong ongoing na project din po sa free Wi-Fi

**[00:51:11.340] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000439)  
to connect po yung 220 state universities and colleges nationwide for Phase 1

**[00:51:17.180] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000440)  
and then we'll connect the balance po by next year.

**[00:51:21.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000441)  
Okay.

**[00:51:22.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000442)  
Pwede po bang makahingi ng list of schools and SUCs na hindi pa po nakakabitan?

**[00:51:29.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000443)  
And even yung priority markets?

**[00:51:32.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000444)  
Or public areas na gustong kabitan para po ma-meet yung 15 million target?

**[00:51:37.880] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000445)  
Mr. Chair, we will comply, Mr. Chair.

**[00:51:40.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000446)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:51:41.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000447)  
Mr. Chair, bilang principal author yung Kabataan Partylist,

**[00:51:44.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000448)  
or one of the principal authors of what is now RA 10929,

**[00:51:48.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000449)  
gusto po natin na continuously ay natche-check kung paano po na-implement ito

**[00:51:52.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000450)  
at paano nagiging beneficiary at nahahatid sa kabataan yung connectivity.

**[00:51:58.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000451)  
Bilang at this point, bahagi na siya ng right to information.

**[00:52:02.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000452)  
Or freedom of access of information.

**[00:52:05.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000453)  
Mr. Chair, my next question would be in relation to siguro pagpondo nito,

**[00:52:11.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000454)  
magkano po yung pondo na nire-request?

**[00:52:13.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000455)  
Is it still 5 million?

**[00:52:14.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000456)  
And is that 3% of the BESF?

**[00:52:19.900] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000457)  
For free Wi-Fi, Mr. Chair, the request is about 5 billion pesos, Mr. Chair, from the SUF.

**[00:52:30.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000458)  
And siguro is that yung pinaka-request na po?

**[00:52:33.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000459)  
Kasi naalala ko lang.

**[00:52:34.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000460)  
Last year, 7.5 billion yung request for the Free Public Internet Access Act.

**[00:52:42.000] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000461)  
Mr. Chair, yung sa 5 billion po na-request,

**[00:52:45.000] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000462)  
ito po yung will ensure na yung mga nakabitanan po natin ngayon ng locations,

**[00:52:49.160] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000463)  
masusustain po natin siya for next.

**[00:52:51.000] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000464)  
But if we're going to have to add more locations and add more schools na makukonektan pa,

**[00:52:59.140] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000465)  
mapaprovide na natin ng libre connectivity na reliable,

**[00:53:02.640] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000466)  
we will need to have...

**[00:53:04.560] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000467)  
may synergy naman po kasi tayo with yung mga other NBP or other programs po ng DICT.

**[00:53:13.080] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000468)  
Pero for free Wi-Fi, yung mas direct and mabilis pong impact is

**[00:53:16.740] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000469)  
every peso po na-added sa FPF budget will really translate po agad sa last mile connectivity.

**[00:53:26.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000470)  
Okay, sige.

**[00:53:27.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000471)  
Kung may additional budget request na lang po in relation sa kung paano mas mapupondohan yung mga additional access,

**[00:53:34.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000472)  
points ay pwede rin pong makareceive na lang through this committee ang representation na ito.

**[00:53:40.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000473)  
Mr. Chair, my next question is in relation to social media registration,

**[00:53:45.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000474)  
given na ito po ay isang mainit na usapin,

**[00:53:48.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000475)  
given po yung naging instances of violence in schools,

**[00:53:53.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000476)  
na napadaloy po yung hindi lang communications,

**[00:53:57.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000477)  
pero paano po nahubog sa ganong sitwasyon through social media.

**[00:54:01.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000478)  
At meron pong mga naging dialogues,

**[00:54:03.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000479)  
ang DICT with...

**[00:54:04.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000480)  
well, social media platforms like Meta,

**[00:54:08.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000481)  
kung paano po ito kagawin and through at pagbibigay pa nga po ng national ID

**[00:54:12.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000482)  
para po maikumpirma yung mga ilang detalye ng mga users dito.

**[00:54:18.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000483)  
And many rights groups are concerned about the implementation of social media registration in the country.

**[00:54:22.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000484)  
So pwede po bang makapag-share ang departamento ng specific plan and or facing of the implementation, Mr. Chair?

**[00:54:31.140] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000485)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor,

**[00:54:33.180] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000486)  
background,

**[00:54:34.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000487)  
lang po,

**[00:54:35.540] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000488)  
yung meeting na nangyari sa Singapore,

**[00:54:40.020] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000489)  
kasama po natin si Congresswoman Matibag din to represent the House,

**[00:54:45.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000490)  
nangyari between Meta and Roblox.

**[00:54:49.380] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000491)  
Sinama po natin doon ng National Privacy Commission,

**[00:54:52.060] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000492)  
ang NTC, CICC, and NBI,

**[00:54:55.280] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000493)  
as well as DepEd po.

**[00:54:57.240] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000494)  
Just as a background, the discussion was very comprehensive.

**[00:55:00.980] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000495)  
Marami pong napag-usapan,

**[00:55:02.580] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000496)  
hindi lang naman yung identity violation,

**[00:55:04.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000497)  
doon sa tanong nyo po na identity verification as it relates to the national ID.

**[00:55:11.820] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000498)  
Si Roblox kasi,

**[00:55:13.680] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000499)  
karamihan ng mga stakeholder niya, mga bata.

**[00:55:18.180] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000500)  
Ang isang problema ni Roblox kasi is age verification.

**[00:55:21.720] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000501)  
Para lang ma-verify yung age nila,

**[00:55:24.980] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000502)  
magpapa facial, I mean facial recognition.

**[00:55:28.000] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000503)  
Facial recognition, tapos yung software nila,

**[00:55:31.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000504)  
plus minus one, two years.

**[00:55:34.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000505)  
May tatlong klase kasi ng Roblox,

**[00:55:37.320] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000506)  
meron yung nine and below,

**[00:55:39.320] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000507)  
meron yung nine to sixteen, and sixteen and above.

**[00:55:43.680] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000508)  
Ang napag-usapan namin,

**[00:55:45.060] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000509)  
paano mas magiging accurate yung age verification?

**[00:55:49.800] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000510)  
Meron kasing tinatawag na i-verify solution ang national ID.

**[00:55:54.820] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000511)  
So kung nandoon na po ang national ID nyo,

**[00:55:57.520] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000512)  
sa nandoon na ang national ID nyo,

**[00:56:00.820] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000513)  
kahit nawala pa yung physical card,

**[00:56:02.820] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000514)  
basta yung digital,

**[00:56:04.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000515)  
biometrics nyo nandoon na sa Philippine Statistical Authority,

**[00:56:08.320] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000516)  
pwede na sila mag-API call.

**[00:56:10.320] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000517)  
So ang mangyari si Roblox, tatanungin si national ID,

**[00:56:14.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000518)  
eto bang si Juan de la Cruz na sinasabing nine years old,

**[00:56:17.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000519)  
na eto yung address niya, et cetera, siya ba yan?

**[00:56:20.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000520)  
Mag-yes-no lang yun.

**[00:56:22.060] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000521)  
So wala pong ililipat na personal information from PSA

**[00:56:27.060] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000522)  
papunta sa Roblox.

**[00:56:28.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000523)  
Ang mangyayari lang is ibe-verify lang na tama yung tao na nagpa,

**[00:56:33.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000524)  
Siyempre pag

**[00:56:34.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000525)  
sinabi niyang nine years old yan, Juan de la Cruz,

**[00:56:37.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000526)  
tapos ang edad pala niya doon sa national ID 16,

**[00:56:41.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000527)  
ang lalabas na sagot doon, no?

**[00:56:44.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000528)  
So yun lang po yung gagwing ni Roblox.

**[00:56:47.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000529)  
Ngayon, kapag gumana po yan sa Roblox,

**[00:56:51.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000530)  
yun naman ang sinasabi ni Meta na po pwede niyang gamitin

**[00:56:55.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000531)  
as another tool para makapag-verify din po siya

**[00:56:59.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000532)  
ng edad ng mga kabataan or iba pang mga information na baka,

**[00:57:03.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000533)  
kailangan nilang makalap.

**[00:57:06.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000534)  
Ito naman po yung nakafocus on making sure na kung sino man yung user,

**[00:57:11.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000535)  
age-appropriate yung content na makukuha niya po.

**[00:57:14.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000536)  
Your Honor.

**[00:57:15.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000537)  
Mr. Chair, just today ay may essentially statement or update ang Meta.

**[00:57:21.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000538)  
Sinabi niya may quote-unquote,

**[00:57:23.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000539)  
technical complexity for integrating the Philippine national ID for user verification.

**[00:57:27.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000540)  
Ano po ba yung dini-detail ng Meta dito na

**[00:57:31.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000541)  
kahirapan,

**[00:57:32.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000542)  
if ever?

**[00:57:33.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000543)  
Yung sinabi naman nila eh, may intindihan po natin.

**[00:57:35.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000544)  
Hindi pa nila nagagawa yan anywhere in the world.

**[00:57:37.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000545)  
First time sa Philippines magagawa yan na mag-i-integrate sila into sovereign national ID.

**[00:57:44.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000546)  
Ang kabalang ni Meta, pagkaginawa namin yan sa isang bansa,

**[00:57:48.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000547)  
gagawin namin yan sa 170 or so countries.

**[00:57:52.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000548)  
So, naiintindihan namin complexity.

**[00:57:54.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000549)  
Kaya ang sagot ni Meta, magpapadala po siya ng technical working team dito in the next two weeks to study.

**[00:58:00.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000550)  
Kaya naman po importante sa amin yung Roblox.

**[00:58:05.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000551)  
Si Roblox po ang nag-suggest noong national ID.

**[00:58:09.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000552)  
Kung babalikan po natin yung meeting, yung unang-unang presentation sa amin ni Roblox eh sinabi nila,

**[00:58:16.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000553)  
oh, baka pwede nating gamitin yung field CIS.

**[00:58:19.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000554)  
Kaya umaasa kami na pagka naging successful si Roblox dyan,

**[00:58:24.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000555)  
baka yan din po yung maging technical template and policy template for the other social media platforms.

**[00:58:30.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000556)  
To follow.

**[00:58:31.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000557)  
Your Honor.

**[00:58:32.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000558)  
Last question, Mr. Chair.

**[00:58:34.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000559)  
Last question.

**[00:58:35.050] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000559-split-2)  
Thank you.

**[00:58:35.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000560)  
Mr. Chair, because today as well, may statement ang Philippine National Police Anti-Cybercrime Group na sinabi po niya

**[00:58:46.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000561)  
that the SIM Registration Act has failed in its original promise of ending cybercrime and scams through SMS.

**[00:58:52.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000562)  
Ineffective daw siya stating that many recent cybercrimes like online selling and scams

**[00:58:58.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000563)  
still rely on registered SIM cards.

**[00:59:01.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000564)  
So meron pong bagong ditong proposal for registration.

**[00:59:08.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000565)  
Ayaw nating essentially mapaso kumpara doon sa kung paano nangyari yung sa SIM card registration

**[00:59:15.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000566)  
na ang citation po ng mga implementing agencies ay it failed in its original promise and premise.

**[00:59:21.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000567)  
Siguro ano pong panghahawakan natin, paano po ma-assure ng department na yung mga problems such as for example,

**[00:59:27.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000568)  
yung trolls, fake accounts to access social media sites or platforms na hindi dapat ma-access ng mga ganong nasa edad,

**[00:59:35.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000569)  
mare-resol ba when other registration avenues and policies have failed in solving these existing problems?

**[00:59:46.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000570)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor, yung SIM registration po didn't live up to its full promise.

**[00:59:54.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000571)  
Nung nandun po ako sa private sector at lumabas,

**[00:59:57.560] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000572)  
lumabas yung SIM registration, tuwang-tuwa po kami diyan.

**[01:00:00.000] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000573)  
Kasi as a banker, ang kalaban po namin yung mga mules and alam nyo na po yung ginagawa ng mga mules, false identity sila. Kaya nung linabas po yung SIM registration, masaya kami sa banko. For a while, it worked.

**[01:00:15.300] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000574)  
Ang naging problema lang sa SIM registration, ang daming IDs na pwedeng gamitin. Labing pitupu ata yun kung di ako nagkakamali. Kami naman po ngayon sa NTC, kasama po sila Commissioner Ella, nag-iisip na baka dapat bawasan natin yung type of IDs.

**[01:00:32.720] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000575)  
Baka dapat sa tatlo lang na national ID ang isa, passport at saka license. Kasi yung mga ibang IDs medyo...

**[01:00:41.720] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000576)  
So, yun po, iiwasan natin na maulit yan sa paggamit ng identity verification, age verification on social media platform by sticking to the national ID.

**[01:00:57.480] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000577)  
Kasi pag national ID na yan, medyo mahirap mo nang i-fake kasi. So, yun po yung balak namin gawin. Limited to an ID that has a higher chance na tama yung tao at saka tama yung information.

**[01:01:11.720] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000578)  
na nandoon po. Your Honor.

**[01:01:14.260] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000579)  
Thank you very much.

**[01:01:15.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000580)  
Thank you very much. So, as a final manifestation, we listened to the groups na may privacy issues. Ang current answer, at least for now, ay that data won't be stored anywhere else other than government.

**[01:01:29.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000581)  
Kasi verification of the details lang yung ibibigay. So, we'd like to further discuss paano ma-insure na yun yung mangyari.

**[01:01:41.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000582)  
I understand na sa dialogue phase pa ang everything at hindi pa ito ang siguradong mangyayari. So, Mr. Chair, siguro I'm looking forward na lang i-hash out itong mga policies na ito na ang punto po ay hindi po mag-i-intrude doon sa right to privacy ng mga mga maya.

**[01:02:00.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000583)  
Sige, Mr. Chair. Thank you.

**[01:06:42.740] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000628)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor, concrete timelines po.

**[01:06:46.780] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000629)  
Ang sinabi namin kay ROBLOX, no later than October, they should comply.

**[01:06:53.020] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000630)  
For the committee's appreciation, there were five demands we gave to ROBLOX since May pa.

**[01:06:59.600] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000631)  
So one, dapat i-segment na nila yung platform nila.

**[01:07:07.080] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000632)  
Ginawa nilang ROBLOX for Kids, ROBLOX Select, tapos ROBLOX.

**[01:07:11.540] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000633)  
Wala po yan dati.

**[01:07:12.900] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000634)  
Noong May, na kinol natin yung attention nila, tumugman naman sila.

**[01:07:16.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000635)  
So linagay nila.

**[01:07:17.440] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000636)  
Yung second, na nag-comply sila, yung chat.

**[01:07:20.840] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000637)  
Yung chat ng mga bata doon, ano yun, binabantayan po ng ROBLOX yun.

**[01:07:25.340] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000638)  
So makikita nila yung mga keywords na related to NVE, related to sexual acts, etc.

**[01:07:32.440] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000639)  
Malalaman nila kung ano yung pinag-usapan ng mga bata.

**[01:07:36.340] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000640)  
Kaya minsan,

**[01:07:37.080] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000641)  
yung mga kabataan, hindi doon sa ROBLOX nagplaplano.

**[01:07:40.060] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000642)  
Lumalabas sila ng another chat.

**[01:07:42.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000643)  
Pangatlo, yung parental control, default na po sa ROBLOX.

**[01:07:46.760] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000644)  
Yung dalawa na kailangan nila mag-comply, yung magtayo ng opisina sa Pilipinas,

**[01:07:51.960] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000645)  
pero sinared na po nila sa amin, meron na po silang hinare na 300 people,

**[01:07:56.600] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000646)  
not through ROBLOX directly, but through a partner of ROBLOX.

**[01:08:01.140] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000647)  
Nagtayo na rin po sila ng opisina sa Singapore.

**[01:08:05.100] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000648)  
Dati po, walang opisina ROBLOX sa Singapore.

**[01:08:07.080] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000649)  
Inuna muna nila yung Singapore, may lima na silang empleyado.

**[01:08:11.120] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000650)  
Tapos isusunod na nila yung sa Pilipinas.

**[01:08:14.080] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000651)  
Yung fifth na lang nakulang is yung age verification through the national ID.

**[01:08:18.920] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000652)  
So by October, dapat o kompleto na po si ROBLOX.

**[01:08:23.160] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000653)  
Again, I keep repeating ROBLOX kasi they can become the template for the other social media platform.

**[01:08:28.480] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000654)  
Kung nagawa nila, yung iba dapat magawa rin.

**[01:08:32.040] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000655)  
Yung kay Meta naman po, ang pangako pa lang nila is on the enforcement side.

**[01:08:37.920] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000656)  
Yung tinatawag nating attribution.

**[01:08:40.240] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000657)  
Pag may gumawang kalukuhan sa Facebook, dapat malaman ng authorities natin sino yun.

**[01:08:46.940] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000658)  
I think they will fly in next week together with TWG.

**[01:08:52.180] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000659)  
Bubuo na sila ng TWG together with NBI and the police.

**[01:09:02.080] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000661)

**[01:09:16.100] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000664)  
alam na nila yung parating na legislation.

**[01:09:18.320] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000665)

**[01:09:20.600] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000666)

**[01:09:21.840] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000667)  
So Meta, realistically, baka before end of the year sila either tumupad o ma-violate nila yung deadline.

**[01:09:30.440] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000668)

But with that, Secretary,

**[01:09:37.080] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-000671)  
convince them na talagang magkaroon ng office

**[01:13:18.830] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-000701)

**[01:13:29.850] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-000702)

**[01:13:38.150] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-000704)

**[01:13:46.270] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000705)  
Thank you, Kong. Kong Anne brings up a really good point. I think we're being really too nice to Meta when it comes to the way we engage with them.

**[01:13:54.990] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000706)  
You know, realistically, the Philippines is the fifth largest audience on Meta. Globally, we make up 3% of Meta's online population.

**[01:14:03.370] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000707)  
That means one out of every 32 users on Meta is Filipino.

**[01:14:08.150] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000708)  
And yet, we play by their rules as if, and they toss us aside as if we're not important to them.

**[01:14:14.330] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000709)  
I don't understand why that's the case. Whereas, with X, when we threatened to shut down X in the Philippines because of the content they were allowing,

**[01:14:24.250] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000710)  
we're only number 12 in X's rankings, only 1.7% of their population, and yet X immediately responded with better safety guidelines and regulations.

**[01:14:36.270] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000711)  
So, I feel that the...

**[01:14:38.150] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000712)  
The ICT moving forward has to really put its foot down already with Meta because it is influencing our children, it is influencing the youth of this nation,

**[01:14:48.410] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000713)  
and it is also influencing the political discourse of our country, at which point it is more of a question of sovereignty and the future of this nation.

**[01:14:58.090] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000714)  
That is a big responsibility which I...

**[01:15:00.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000715)  
feel that DICT should make

**[01:15:01.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000716)  
Meta feel. That's all.

**[01:15:03.820] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000717)  
Sorry I had to interject,

**[01:15:06.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000718)

**[01:22:27.610] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000934)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor,

**[01:22:29.590] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000935)  
with your permission, if I could

**[01:22:31.550] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000936)
