**[01:22:33.710] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000937)  
Aguirre to respond with

**[01:22:35.490] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000938)  
the more accurate information

**[01:22:37.750] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000939)  
as he is also the one in charge of the

**[01:22:39.350] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-000940)  
program, Mr. Chair.

**[01:22:40.850] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000941)  
Mr. Aguirre, you're recognized.

**[01:22:43.190] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000942)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:22:45.970] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000943)  
In relation sa

**[01:22:47.170] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000944)  
DICT Digital Workforce Hub, hindi pa po

**[01:22:49.370] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000945)  
siya nasisimulan, Mr. Chair.

**[01:22:51.990] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000946)  
We will be launching it

**[01:22:53.350] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000947)  
this Wednesday.

**[01:22:55.650] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000948)  
The idea for the DICT Digital Workforce Hub,

**[01:22:57.790] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000949)  
Mr. Chair, is that it

**[01:22:59.410] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000950)  
provides an ecosystem

**[01:23:01.870] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000951)  
where those that are looking for jobs

**[01:23:03.810] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000952)  
will be able to

**[01:23:05.250] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000953)  
be

**[01:23:07.570] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000954)  
they will be able to find

**[01:23:09.310] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000955)  
these jobs more easily.

**[01:23:11.990] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000956)  
What it is

**[01:23:13.490] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000957)  
envisioned to look like is

**[01:23:15.270] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000958)  
that the different employers

**[01:23:17.510] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000959)  
will upload their detailed job descriptions

**[01:23:19.410] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000960)  
on the platform.

**[01:23:21.410] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000961)  
Those that are looking for jobs will upload

**[01:23:23.350] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000962)  
their CVs. We will give accounts free

**[01:23:25.310] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000963)  
of charge to everyone that is interested.

**[01:23:28.050] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000964)  
Based on the

**[01:23:29.270] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000965)  
analysis of the platform

**[01:23:31.070] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000966)  
using artificial intelligence,

**[01:23:33.490] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000967)  
it will point you, based on your current

**[01:23:35.490] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000968)  
CV, these are the jobs

**[01:23:37.470] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000969)  
that you're qualified for. If there are

**[01:23:39.270] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000970)  
gaps, it will direct you

**[01:23:41.130] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000971)  
to where you can avail of these

**[01:23:43.190] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000972)  
trainings. You can take the trainings

**[01:23:45.170] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000973)  
on the platform itself using

**[01:23:47.410] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000974)  
the courseware that DICT

**[01:23:49.310] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000975)  
has developed. It can also point

**[01:23:51.450] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000976)  
you to external partners that

**[01:23:53.230] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000977)  
have integrated their learning

**[01:23:55.050] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000978)  
management systems. Then, there are

**[01:23:57.230] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000979)  
micro-credentials that can be obtained

**[01:23:59.310] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000980)  
from those. In this way, it will be

**[01:24:01.350] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000981)  
easier for us to monitor

**[01:24:03.430] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000982)  
kung sino yung mga nakakakuha

**[01:24:05.350] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000983)  
ng trabaho. Kasi kahit po

**[01:24:07.350] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000984)  
ngayon, marami na tayong

**[01:24:09.110] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000985)  
na-train. Yung reporting po

**[01:24:11.090] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000986)  
kung sino sa mga na-train

**[01:24:13.130] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000987)  
yung actually nakakuha ng trabaho,

**[01:24:15.450] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000988)  
medyo mahirap po kasi

**[01:24:17.130] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000989)  
wala namang obligasyon doon sa part ng

**[01:24:19.070] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000990)  
mga na-train na mag-report na

**[01:24:20.790] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000991)  
nakakuha na sila ng trabaho. Yun po

**[01:24:22.730] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000992)  
yung gusto naming solusyunan

**[01:24:25.250] DICT Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Y. Aguirre** (asr-000993)  
through the Digital Workforce Hub, Mr. Chair.

**[01:42:10.110] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001491)

**[01:42:54.960] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-001499)

**[01:43:02.480] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-001500)

**[01:43:09.540] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-001501)

**[01:43:27.180] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-001504)

**[01:43:34.280] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-001505)

**[01:43:39.540] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-001506)

**[01:43:47.200] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-001507)

**[01:44:19.400] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001517)

**[01:44:23.200] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001518)

**[01:44:35.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001520)

**[01:45:46.640] DICT Undersecretary for Support Services Heherson M. Asiddao** (asr-001530)

**[01:46:11.540] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001539)  
Free Public Internet Access Program receives 5 billion in 2026.

**[01:46:16.640] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001540)  
Out of this, you reported 29,091 active free Wi-Fi points in 11,836 locations nationwide.

**[01:46:30.360] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001541)  
However, COA identified several deficiencies in appropriate or missing signages,

**[01:46:38.040] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001542)  
inadequate instructions for connecting, inadequate awareness activities in operative sites in NCR and Region 5,

**[01:46:46.640] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001543)  
limited internet hours in Region 5, issues involving speed and reliability, delayed resolution of complaints.

**[01:46:58.220] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001544)

**[01:47:39.080] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001555)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:47:40.280] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001556)  
Mr. Chair, you're correct.

**[01:47:41.840] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001557)  
We have 11,836 currently locations that we're covering with the FDIC.

**[01:47:46.640] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001558)

**[01:47:54.560] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001559)  
So, to monitor each access point, we have a network monitoring system in our agency that we maintain and monitor.

**[01:48:05.200] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001560)

**[01:48:11.140] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001561)  
and within 72 hours, it will...

**[01:48:14.760] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001562)  
The...

**[01:48:15.420] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001563)  
Kong...

**[01:48:16.640] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001564)

**[01:48:19.720] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001565)

**[01:48:31.200] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001566)

**[01:48:33.740] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001568)

**[01:48:35.260] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001569)

**[01:48:39.200] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001570)

**[01:48:43.400] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001571)

**[01:48:44.760] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001572)

**[01:48:46.640] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-001573)

**[01:49:30.810] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001582)

**[01:49:38.550] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-001583)

**[01:49:40.770] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-001584)

**[01:49:46.070] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-001585)

**[01:49:52.910] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-001586)

**[01:49:56.370] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-001587)  
Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[01:49:59.710] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001589)

**[01:50:31.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001605)  
I just want to point out something. I got a phone in from one of our deputy speakers who pointed out something important. We have 61 million downloads, 56 million verified accounts, and 1.2 billion transactions on eGov. But I just realized eGov is done all in-house. No, there's no contractors involved with eGov. What happens when you're no longer in position?

**[01:50:56.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001606)  
Will we be able to continue this service? Or we have a continuity problem here, if ever?

**[01:51:04.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001607)  
Yusek, you're recognized. I know you're in charge of this program.

**[01:51:08.780] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001608)  
Mr. Chair, thank you for that question.

**[01:51:11.660] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001609)  
So, yes, Mr. Chair, it's true that the e-gov super app is in-house developed, meaning we don't have contractors.

**[01:51:19.500] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001610)  
And, in fact, this is the reason also maybe we need help from the Congress to help us sustain this.

**[01:51:26.280] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001611)  
And maybe the biggest issue here is salary, if we're able to, because the salary in government is too low.

**[01:51:33.540] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001612)  
And I think it's now about time to come up with a solution. How could we bring the IT community and even programmers to come and join the government?

**[01:51:44.340] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001613)  
We don't have contractors, Mr. Chair, because in the past, we know that we don't want to be tied up with like a hostage of system or technology.

**[01:51:53.660] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001614)  
But since there's a new law,

**[01:51:56.140] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001615)  
Mr. Chair, the e-government law,

**[01:51:58.540] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001616)  
the e-gov law is another office under the ICT to sustain this.

**[01:52:03.140] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001617)  
And there's also a Magna Carta embedded to that to add salary to the IT people joining the government.

**[01:52:09.660] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001618)  
But, honest to goodness, Mr. Chair, it's not tantamount to the private salary because private salary is too high versus the government.

**[01:52:18.380] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001619)  
So, that is, Mr. Chair, our plan of sustainability.

**[01:52:24.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001620)  
So, there is a guarantee.

**[01:52:26.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001621)  
The guarantee of continuity, it's just the number of personnel and funding that it would receive.

**[01:52:30.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001622)  
But there is a guarantee that the e-gov app will be able to transition from administration to administration.

**[01:52:36.660] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001623)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:52:37.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001624)  
Okay. Just wanted to get that clarity.

**[01:52:40.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001625)

ng

ng gobyerno,

**[03:43:50.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005237)

**[03:43:55.650] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005239)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair. With respect to

**[03:43:57.850] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005240)  
the National Grid Corporation, I'm not sure if

**[03:43:59.850] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005241)  
it has personal data.

**[03:44:01.990] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005242)  
Yung coverage po ng

**[03:44:03.390] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005243)  
RA 10173, or the Data Privacy Act,

**[03:44:05.850] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005244)  
is not all forms of data, but what we call

**[03:44:08.070] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005245)  
personal data, personal information, or

**[03:44:10.090] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005246)  
sensitive personal information

**[03:44:11.590] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005247)  
of individual data subjects.

**[03:44:14.170] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005248)  
So, if it were personal data

**[03:44:15.930] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005249)  
of, for example, employees already ng NGCP

**[03:44:18.210] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005250)  
or potential customers ng NGCP,

**[03:44:20.070] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005251)  
if they have individual customers, then

**[03:44:21.970] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005252)  
that would already be covered by the data

**[03:44:23.850] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005253)  
privacy act.

**[03:44:26.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005253-split-2)  
Other than that,

**[03:44:28.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005254)  
other data na

**[03:44:29.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005255)  
hindi personal,

**[03:44:31.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005256)  
po pwede. If it's

**[03:44:34.760] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005257)  
non-personal data, Mr. Chair, that's outside

**[03:44:36.880] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005258)  
of the purview already of RA 10173,

**[03:44:38.700] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005259)  
so I cannot say whether or not

**[03:44:41.080] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005260)  
yan or not.

**[03:44:42.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005261)  
Alimbawa, Mr. Chair, yung data

**[03:44:44.840] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005262)  
ng mga planta

**[03:44:47.100] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005263)  
na ginagamit

**[03:44:49.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005264)  
sa isang klaseng servisyo,

**[03:44:53.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005265)  
no need na kung

**[03:44:54.380] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005266)  
kasi apat na klase yung

**[03:44:56.780] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005267)  
in the power industry yung ano natin,

**[03:44:59.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005268)  
mga tumatakbo.

**[03:45:00.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005269)  
Yung mga planta na nagkaroon ng mga outages, so is that personal based on your...

**[03:45:09.760] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005270)  
It would not be personal data, Mr. Chair.

**[03:45:12.780] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005271)  
That would actually form part possibly of statistics, statistics and numbers data for the operation of the NGCP.

**[03:45:18.840] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005272)  
But personally, if it's government data, the NGCP is a concessionaire of the National Transmission Corporation.

**[03:45:24.580] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005273)  
So actually, in my opinion, there should be actually visibility on the part of the national government with respect to data of its concessionaires.

**[03:45:33.880] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005274)  
Mr. Chair, is the NPC aware of our electricity market operation? Are you aware of that?

**[03:45:44.960] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005275)  
As an institution, Mr. Chair, we're not familiar, we're not privy to electricity market operations because that's not within the mandate of the NPC.

**[03:45:54.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005276)  
But...

**[03:45:54.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005277)  
But, for example, the data within the wholesale electricity spot market, is that personal or the data that can be shared?

**[03:46:06.060] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005278)  
For the wholesale spot electricity market, in my understanding, it does not contain personal data.

**[03:46:12.360] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005279)  
Personal data with respect to the distribution utilities possibly because distribution utilities would have customer information.

**[03:46:19.760] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005280)  
And in that regard, the distribution utilities would be personal information controllers.

**[03:46:24.560] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005281)  
covered by the Data Privacy Act.

**[03:46:25.860] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-005282)  
But to my understanding, Mr. Chair, those who are part of the WESEM, I don't think they trade in personal data, they trade in electricity.

**[03:46:34.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005283)  
Thank you, thank you, NPC.

**[03:46:38.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005284)

**[03:46:45.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005285)

**[03:47:02.040] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-005288)

**[03:47:09.360] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-005289)

**[03:47:18.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005290)

**[03:47:24.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005291)

**[03:47:29.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005292)

**[03:47:32.940] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-005293)

**[03:47:38.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005294)

**[03:47:42.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005295)

**[03:47:48.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005296)

**[03:47:50.220] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005297)  
May I ask, NTC, Mr. Chair,

**[03:47:53.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005298)  
may no monitor nyo po ba yung connectivity doon sa mga probinsya,

**[03:47:58.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005299)

**[03:47:59.280] NTC Commissioner Ella Blanca B. Lopez** (asr-005300)  
Mr. Chair, mayroon po kaming validation sa regional offices.

**[03:48:07.480] NTC Commissioner Ella Blanca B. Lopez** (asr-005301)  
Yes po, may mamonitor po, Mr. Chair.

**[03:48:10.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005302)  
With all due respect, hindi po totoo yun na binabalidit ninyo.

**[03:48:18.920] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005303)  
Siargao ang dinagat, habang tumatagal,

**[03:48:22.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005304)  
nagdadagdag kami pag-connect ng mga seal sites,

**[03:48:26.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005305)  
lalong nawawala yung signal.

**[03:48:28.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005306)  
Kaya nga, lahat ng nag-a-apply ng aplikasyon,

**[03:48:31.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005307)  
sa siaril ko at saka sa delco,

**[03:48:34.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005308)  
bago ko i-connect, pinapapirma ko ng undertaking

**[03:48:37.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005309)  
na kapag ka hindi nag-improve yung signal sa opisina namin,

**[03:48:41.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005310)  
hirap na hirap na kami doon sa signal namin.

**[03:48:43.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005311)  
Dagdag nang dagdag kayo ng ano.

**[03:48:46.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005312)  
So, that's why, with all due respect, akin,

**[03:48:49.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005313)  
hindi po totoo na binabalidit ninyo.

**[03:48:52.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005314)  
Sino-sino po yung mga tao ng NPC na pumupunta doon?

**[03:48:56.500] NTC Commissioner Ella Blanca B. Lopez** (asr-005315)  
Mr. Chair, we'll have to check po,

**[03:48:58.840] NTC Commissioner Ella Blanca B. Lopez** (asr-005316)  
specifically sa region po,

**[03:49:01.320] NTC Commissioner Ella Blanca B. Lopez** (asr-005317)  
sa Siargao and sa Dinagat,

**[03:49:02.480] NTC Commissioner Ella Blanca B. Lopez** (asr-005318)  
but we do have a validation po, Mr. Chair,

**[03:49:05.340] NTC Commissioner Ella Blanca B. Lopez** (asr-005319)  
sa mga regional offices and regular po yun na ginagawa.

**[03:49:09.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005320)  
Malayo po yung Butuan, no?

**[03:49:11.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005321)  
Ito yung taga Butuan.

**[03:49:13.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005322)  
Paano nila binabalidit kung nasa Butuan lang sila

**[03:49:15.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005323)  
or sa Surigao City,

**[03:49:17.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005324)  
kung hindi sila tatawi doon sa Siargao at Dinagat,

**[03:49:20.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005325)

**[03:49:24.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005326)  
Even ako dumadaan sa Commonwealth,

**[03:49:27.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005327)  
Commonwealth na, nawawala pa yung signal.

**[03:49:29.260] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005328)  
That's why, kindly,

**[03:49:31.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005329)  
no, NTC naman,

**[03:49:33.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005330)  
nagbabayad po tayo.

**[03:49:35.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005331)  
Hindi po to katulad ng korinti na

**[03:49:37.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005332)  
kapag kawalang korinti, hindi ikot yung metro.

**[03:49:40.220] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005333)  
Ito po ay sa bahay ko lang sa Siargao.

**[03:49:43.980] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005334)  
Kaya magdita ka na lang kasi nagbabayad ka naman monthly.

**[03:49:47.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005335)  
Fix yun.

**[03:49:47.860] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005336)  
Tapos dalawang araw, apat na araw.

**[03:49:52.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005337)  
So that's why I'm telling you, Mr. Chair,

**[03:49:55.400] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005338)

**[04:17:36.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006073)

**[04:17:42.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006075)

**[04:17:49.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006076)

**[04:17:53.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006077)

**[04:17:56.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006078)

**[04:17:58.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006079)

**[04:17:59.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006080)  
in our government?

**[04:18:00.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006081)  
I know that DBM has UACS codes.

**[04:18:02.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006082)

**[04:18:06.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006083)

**[04:18:16.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006084)

**[04:18:20.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006085)

**[04:18:24.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006086)

**[04:18:28.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006087)

**[04:18:30.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006088)

**[04:18:35.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006089)  
Mr. Chair, and thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[04:18:39.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006090)  
Now I wanted to ask, because data standards are important.

**[04:18:45.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006091)  
When we look at DBM as well, it should be, you know, may UACS yan, unified identifiers.

**[04:18:51.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006092)  
Would we go from the formulation of the NEP to the MGA, to SAROs, disbursements?

**[04:18:57.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006093)

**[04:19:07.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006094)  
And I know, I'm a bit exposed about blockchain.

**[04:19:13.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006095)  
And we all know that it is a ledger, a distributed ledger, that is encrypted by a cryptographic hash.

**[04:19:20.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006096)  
I wanted to ask, because I know that this is being pushed as well.

**[04:19:25.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006097)  
Do you think that...

**[04:19:26.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006098)

**[04:19:36.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006099)

**[04:19:40.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006100)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor, I think the concept of Kadena, the CA, the Kadena bill, is feasible in our government.

**[04:19:49.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006101)  
As to the amount, I think it depends on how the legislature will craft the laws.

**[04:19:54.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006102)  
Whether it's public chain, private chain, and the extent to which it will be implemented.

**[04:20:02.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006103)  
But I think whatever the amount is, if it achieves the objective of transparency in government, it will be worth it, Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[04:20:11.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006104)  
Mr. Chair and Mr. Secretary, do you believe that Kadena will solve corruption in the Philippines?

**[04:20:17.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006105)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor, Kadena is a tool to curb corruption.

**[04:20:22.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006106)  
As with all transparency portals that we're implementing right now.

**[04:20:28.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006107)  
Ultimately, it's how effective the tool is implemented.

**[04:20:32.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006108)  
I think it can minimize corruption.

**[04:20:36.200] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006109)  
Definitely, it will increase transparency.

**[04:20:39.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006110)  
Thank you.

**[04:20:40.200] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006111)  
And when we talk about Kadena,

**[04:20:49.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006112)  
and it's supposed to curb corruption,

**[04:20:52.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006113)  
it's supposed to curb corruption, I understand.

**[04:20:57.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006114)  
But I know that Bayani Chain has also made on-chain for free.

**[04:21:03.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006115)  
Do you believe that this is possible?

**[04:21:06.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006116)  
That we can also have a very minimal budget for this in case it does pass or in case that we do pursue it?

**[04:21:15.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006117)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor,

**[04:21:18.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006118)  
the one that Bayani Chain did was a free chain.

**[04:21:24.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006119)  
Basically, what they did is just onboarded the published budget of 2025 into a public chain.

**[04:21:36.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006120)  
I think the approach of uploading the published budget on any chain is feasible and that's free.

**[04:21:46.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006121)  
The question there is if you establish a system end-to-end from NEP to expenditure.

**[04:21:52.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006122)  
And more importantly, which is I'm a big proponent of,

**[04:21:55.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006123)  
you use stable coin.

**[04:21:57.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006124)  
Because if you want to track every peso that comes out of the government,

**[04:22:02.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006125)  
the easier way of doing it is to just tokenize all of the peso.

**[04:22:06.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006126)  
And then, you can now follow the provenance.

**[04:22:09.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006127)  
Now, if that's the ambition that we want,

**[04:22:13.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006128)  
I think that would be the better solution.

**[04:22:15.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006129)  
Because right now, each citizen can account for every peso that goes out of the government.

**[04:22:20.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006130)  
But it will take a lot of work, Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[04:22:24.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006131)  
So it seems, Mr. Chair and Mr. Secretary,

**[04:22:26.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006132)  
it seems that once we make it transparent,

**[04:22:31.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006133)

**[04:22:38.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006134)

**[04:22:42.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006135)  
And I think I wanted to ask,

**[04:22:45.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006136)

**[04:22:49.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006137)

**[04:22:54.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006138)

**[04:22:58.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006139)

**[04:23:02.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006140)

**[04:23:08.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006141)  
we're the custodian and owner of the source code at this point.

**[04:23:12.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006142)

**[04:23:13.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006143)

**[04:23:15.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006144)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor, maybe I should turn it over to USEC Admiral for the details

**[04:23:20.500] DICT Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda** (asr-006145)  
so that it's consistent with his earlier statement.

**[04:23:25.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006146)  
Okay.

**[04:23:26.500] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006147)  
USEC, you may share the numbers once more?

**[04:23:28.500] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006148)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[04:23:29.500] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006149)  
Let me answer that.

**[04:23:30.500] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006150)  
So right now, Mr. Chair, since we already have 61 million downloads with 56 million unique users,

**[04:23:37.500] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006151)  
our average daily download, Mr. Chair, is about 70,000 to 100,000 a day.

**[04:23:43.500] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006152)  
So that's the average download.

**[04:23:44.500] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006153)  
So approximately, Mr. Chair, for monthly active downloads,

**[04:23:50.500] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006154)  
about around 3 million downloads monthly, Mr. Chair.

**[04:23:58.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006155)  
Okay.

**[04:23:59.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006156)  
Mr. Chair and USEC, how much is that per user, per transaction is the cost to run EGov?

**[04:24:10.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006157)  
In terms of maybe economic impact on the current budget that we have,

**[04:24:15.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006158)  
approximately, Mr. Chair, around 9 pesos per user, Mr. Chair.

**[04:24:19.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006159)  
Okay.

**[04:24:20.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006160)  
And how does EGov get funded?

**[04:24:23.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006161)  
For the development, Mr. Chair, since we build this internally,

**[04:24:27.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006162)  
so we don't have any contractor developing and building EGov.

**[04:24:31.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006163)  
It's an in-house developed platform.

**[04:24:33.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006164)  
Right now, Mr. Chair, we're only paying our own staff to do it.

**[04:24:38.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006165)  
Right now, we have about approximately more than 30 programmers

**[04:24:44.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006166)  
doing it, Mr. Chair.

**[04:24:47.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006167)  
Okay. So, Mr. Chair, you said 9 pesos per user, correct?

**[04:24:53.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006168)  
For comparing our current budget, EGov budget, to the users, Mr. Chair.

**[04:24:59.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006169)  
Okay. So all internal and no line item budget, correct?

**[04:25:04.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006170)  
And there are no private companies or contractors working?

**[04:25:09.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006171)  
Yes, Mr. Chair, for the development, for the R&D.

**[04:25:12.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006172)  
The one that's only paying, Mr. Chair, is actually the subscription of our storage,

**[04:25:16.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006173)  
our cloud, service security, and other non-software developers' expenses.

**[04:25:21.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006174)  
Okay. And where is this cloud stored?

**[04:25:24.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006175)  
Right now, Mr. Chair, it's stored in Singapore.

**[04:25:27.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006176)  
So since we're using public cloud services, Mr. Chair.

**[04:25:31.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006177)  
Okay. So what company in Singapore, if I may ask?

**[04:25:35.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006178)  
So we're using AWS, like 80%.

**[04:25:38.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006179)  
We also have several services stored in Google.

**[04:25:41.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006180)  
Okay.

**[04:25:42.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006181)  
We have some in the Google Cloud and also some also in our current existing co-locations, Mr. Chair.

**[04:25:48.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006182)  
Okay. Mr. Chair, so it seems that the data of our Filipino people are in AWS or Google Cloud in Singapore, no?

**[04:25:56.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006183)  
Mr. Chair, I'd like to ask the department if are you amenable that we have sovereign data centers to house our data?

**[04:26:05.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006184)  
That's the ideal, Mr. Chair.

**[04:26:07.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006185)  
So we're supposed to own it, manage it locally.

**[04:26:11.550] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-006186)  
Locally, Mr. Chair.

**[04:26:13.550] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-006187)  
Okay. Thank you.

With your permission, Mr. Chair.

**[04:26:57.550] DICT Undersecretary for Policy, Legal, and Communications Sarah Maria Q. Sison** (asr-006200)

**[04:27:12.550] DICT Undersecretary for Policy, Legal, and Communications Sarah Maria Q. Sison** (asr-006202)
